# Graph Report - agent-verse-backend  (2026-09-11)

## Corpus Check
- 2947 files · ~2,362,587 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 48356 nodes · 115326 edges · 1571 communities (1201 shown, 93 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 94% EXTRACTED · 6% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6853 edges (avg confidence: 0.94)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `1ed046e2`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
